URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT
Madam Kama Marg, Hutatma Rajguru Chowk,
Mantralaya, Mumbai - 400 032.
Dated 22nd February 2024
The Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966
NOTIFICATION
No. TPS-1815/428/CR-138/15/UD-13.--- Whereas, the Government in Urban Development Department,
under Section 31(1) of the Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966 (Maharashtra Act No.
XXXVII of 1966) (hereinafter referred to as "the said Act") has sanctioned the Revised Development Plan
of the old limit of Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal Corporation vide Notification No. TPS-1893/1276/CR-174/
93/UD-13, dated 18th September 1995 and has sanctioned the Revised Development Plan of Pimpri-
Chinchwad New Town Development Authority vide Notification No. TPS-1893/1412/UD-13, dated 28th
November 1995 and some area under the planning control of Pimpri-Chinchwad New Town Development
Authority has been included under the planning control of Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal Corporation
(hereinafter referred to as "the said Municipal Corporation") vide Notification No. PCN-1697/934/CR-89/UD-
22, dated 15th November 1997. Also Development Plan of extended limits of the Pimpri-Chinchwad
Municipal Corporation has partly sanctioned vide Notification No. TPS-1805/1050/CR-795/05/UD-13, dated
30th May 2008 and Notification No. TPS-1808/894/CR-1727/09/UD-13, dated 18th August 2009 and
sanctioned the Development Plan of Excluded Part vide Notification No. TPS-1812/160/CR-58/12/
Reconstruction No. 27/12/EP-Sanction/UD-13, dated 2nd May 2015 (hereinafter referred to as "the said
Development Plans" and "the said notifications") ;
and whereas, the Government vide Resolution No. TPS-1815/428/CR-138/15/UD-13, dated 15th July
2015 has directed the said Municipal Corporation to initiate modification to the sanctioned Development
plan of extended limits under Section 37 (1) of the said Act, to include the lands falling beyond the Red
flood line shown in No Development Zone to Residential zone ;
and whereas, as per the Government directives, Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal Corporation vide
Resolution No. 824, dated 20 February 2016 has decided to include the lands falling between the blue
flood line and red flood line and lands falling beyond the red flood line shown in No Development Zone
(Green Belt / Zone) to Residential Zone, in the Development Plan of extended limit as well as in the revised
Development Plan of old limits of the said Municipal Corporation, for availability of contiguous Residential
Zone and for planned development point of view (hereinafter referred to as the “said modification”) ;

and whereas, the said Municipal Corporation has submitted the said modification in the said
Development Plan, after following all legal procedure under the provision of section 37 (1) of the said Act,
to the Government vide letter No. 143/2017, dated 7th August 2017 for Sanction;
and whereas, after making necessary enquiries and after consulting the Director of Town Planning,
Maharashtra State, Pune, the Government is of opinion that the said proposed modification needs to be
sanctioned, with some condition ;
and whereas, out of the lands under the said modification, concerned land owners of some lands
have deposited the requisite amount of premium to the Government and the said Municipal Corporation ;
and whereas, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 37 (2) of the said Act, the said
modification proposal has been sanctioned, with some conditions, for the lands out of the lands under the
said modification, as mentioned in Annexure appended to this notification, for which the requisite amount
of premium is deposited with the Government and the said Municipal Corporation by the Government vide
Urban Development Department Notification No. TPS-1815/428/C. R. 138/15/UD-13, dated 17th September
2019, Notification No. TPS-1815/428/C. R. 138/15/UD-13, dated 20th January 2021 and Notification No.
TPS-1815/428/C. R. 138/15/UD-13, dated 8th November 2021 ;
and whereas, out of the lands under the said modification, the Commissioner Pimpri-Chinchwad
lands of which concerned owners have deposited the requisite amount of premium from the notification,
dated 20th January 2021 upto dated 8th March 2021 to the Government and the said Municipal Corporation ;
and whereas, the owner of the land situated at village Chichwad S. No. 17/3 pt., C.T.S. No. 2017
pt., 2018 pt., 2019 pt., 2025 pt. and 2026 pt. has requested the Govenment to issue the Notification
sanctioning the proposal for the land under Section 37 (2) of the said Act vide letter, dated 23rd January
2023 and attached the relevant documents.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 37 (2) of the said Act, the Government
hereby sanctions the said modification proposal partly, with some conditions, and for that purpose amends
the said notifications as follows.
In the schedule of modification appended to the said notifications, after the last entry, following new
entry shall be added viz :---
"ENTRY"
(A) Out of the lands falling between the blue flood line (prohibitive flood line) and red flood line
(Maximum/Controlling flood line) and lands falling beyond the red flood line in the sanctioned Development
Plans of extended limits and of old limits of the Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal Corporation and also in the
Development Plan of Pimpri-Chinchwad New Town Development Authority, which are under planning
control of Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal Corporation, shown in No Development Zone (Green Belt/Zone) are
included in Residential Zone as mentioned in Annexure attached herewith, as shown on plan, subject to
following condition.

Conditions :--- Minimum 15 per cent area should be provided as amenity space in layout having
area 0.40 ha. or more under the said modification. It is binding that development/construction of amenity
in such amenity space should be made, as decided by the Commissioner, Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal
Corporation. It is binding to handover the amenity space to Municipal Corporation for public purpose as
required by the municipal corporation.
(B) The remaining lands out of the said modification, for which the requisite amount of premium is
not deposited and which are not finally sanctioned under Section 37 (2) of the said Act, such lands are
deemed to have been deleted from the process of the said modification and are considered to be
remained in Agricultural Zone.
(i) Part Plan bearing No. TPS-1815/428/C.R.-138/15/UD-13, showing the aforesaid sanctioned
modification is kept open for inspection of the general public in the working hours on all working days in
the office of the Commissioner, Pimpri-Chinchwad Municipal Corporation, Pimpri for the period of one
month.
(ii) This notification is published on government website www.maharashtra.gov.in (Acts/Rules).
Annexure
(Accompaniment to the Government in Urban Development Department Notification
No. TPS-1815/428/C. R. 138/15/UD-13, dated 22nd February 2024).
Sr. No. Name of Village S. No. Areas (Sq. mtrs.)

2025 pt. and 2026 pt.
By order and in the name of the Governor of Maharashtra,
PRANAV KARPE,
Under Secretary to Government.

URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT
4th Floor, Mantralaya, Mumbai - 400 032.
Dated 21st February 2024
The Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966
NOTIFICATION
No. TPS-2122/Court-145/C.R.No.-106/2023/UD-13.–– Whereas, the Revised Development Plan of
Ichalkaranji Municipal Council (Now Ichalkaranji Municipal Corporation) has been partly sanctioned under
the provisions of Section 31(1) of the Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966 (Maharashtra
Act No. XXXVII of 1966) (hereinafter referred to as “the said Act”) by the Government in Urban Development
Department vide its Notification No. TPS-2194/270/CR-22-A/94/UD-13, dated 5th March 1999 (hereinafter
referred to as the “said Notification”) which is came into force with effect from dated 7th May 1999 and
republished Developement Plan of Excluded Part of Ichalkaranji has been sanctioned vide Notification
No. TPS-2100/1429/C.R.-133/2000/UD-13, dated 3rd September 2001 and came into force with effect
from 1st November 2001 (hereinafter referred to as the “said Sanctioned Development Plan”) ;
and whereas, as per the said Sanctioned Development Plan, the part portion of the land bearing
Survey No. 678/A/1/A, Survey No. 678/A/1/B and Survey No. 678/A/1/C, of Kasba-Ichalkaranji, taluka
Hatkanangale, district Kolhapur area admeasuring 0.44 Hectare Are, more particularly described in
Schedule “A” (hereinafter referred to as “the said land”) is Reserved for Site No. 38, “Garden” (hereinafter
referred to as “the said Reservation”) ;
and whereas, the said land has not been acquired by the Ichalkaranji Municipal Council (Now Municipal
Corporation) (hereinafter referred to as “the said Municipal Council”) within ten years from the date on
which the said Development Plan has came into force ;
and whereas, the Owner of the said land served a Notice, dated 31st May 2016 under Section 127(1)
of the said Act to the said Municipal Council (Now Municipal Corporation), being the Planning Authority,
informing that the said land is to be acquired by the said “Municipal Council” (Now “Municipal Corporation”)
within the stipulated period ;
and whereas, even after service of the Notice as aforesaid, the said land has neither been acquired,
nor the Notification of Land Acquisition required under Section 126(4) of the Maharashtra Regional and
Town Planning Act, 1966 read with Section 19 of the Right to Fair Compensation of Transparency in
Land Acquisition, Rehabilitation and Resettlement Act, 2013 had been published by the said Municipal
Council (Now Municipal Corporation) before 31st May 2018 i.e. within a period of two years from the date
of the service of the Notice under sub-section (1) of Section 127 of the said Act ;
and whereas, the Hon'ble High Court of the Judicature of Bombay in Writ Petition No. 8611 of 2021
filed by the Owner of the said Land, has passed Order on 7th December 2021 that the said reservation
on the said land is lapsed and that the area under the said reservation is now available to the Petitioner’s
for development entirely free from any reservation and directed the State Government to issue Notification
under Section 127 (2) of the said Act ;
and whereas, after receiving the Order of Hon'ble High Court, Bombay and report of the said
Municipal Council (Now Municipal Corporation) and report of the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra
State, Pune, the Government of Maharashtra is of the opinion that, the said reservation in the said
Development Plan stands lapsed as per the provisions of Section 127 (1) of the said Act.

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (2) of Section 127 of the said
Act, and of all other powers enabling it in that behalf, the Government of Maharashra hereby notifies that,
Site No. 38, “Garden” in the said Development Plan (more particularly described in the Schedule - ‘A’
hereinbelow,) stand released from the said reservation and such area of the said land will be avaliable to
the Owners for the purpose of Development as otherwise, permissible in the case of adjacent land under
the said sanctioned Development Plan.
A copy of the Part Plan showing the area of the said Reservation which has been lapsed vide this
Order, will be available in the office of the Commissioner, Ichalkaranji Municipal Corporation, district
Kolhapur during office hours on all working days for inspection of public for the period of one month.

BY ADMINISTRATOR AND CHIEF OFFICER
PALUS MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PALUS
Publication of Draft Development Plan of Palus under Section 26 (1) of Maharashtra Regional & Town
Planning Act, 1966
NOTICE
¯Ö»ÖæÃÖ ®Ö¯Ö/284/2024.--- Whereas the Palus Municipal Council, (hereinafter referred to as "the said Planning
Authority.") vide Resolution No. 90, dated 13th December 2023 had declared its intention to prepare a Draft
Development Plan of Palus (hereinafter referred to as the "said Draft Development Plan") for the area

under its jurisdiction, (hereinafter referred to as the said "area") as per the provision of Section 23 (1) read
with Section 21 (2) of Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966 (hereinafter referred to as the
“said Act”) and Notice to that effect is published in Maharashtra Government Gazette Part-1, Pune Division,
Supplement, dated, February 09 - 15, 2023 ;
and whereas, as per the provision in Section 24 of the said Act, the Palus Municipal Council vide
Order No. 5046/2023, dated 17th April 2023 has appointed Assistant Director, Town Planning Sangali
Branch, Sangali as Town Planning officer to prepare the said Draft Development Plan for the said area ;
and whereas, the Town Planning Officer and Assistant Director, Town Plannin, Sangali Branch, Sangali
after having prepared Existing Land Use Map as per Section 25 of the said Act, has prepared the said Draft
Development Plan of the said area and has handed it over to the said Planning Authority on 6th March
2024 for publication under Section 26 (1) of the said Act;
and whereas, the said Planning Authority has passed Resolution No. 224, dated 6th March 2024 to
publish the said Draft Development Plan under Section 26 (1) of the said Act, for inviting suggestions/
objections from the public.
Therefore Palus Municipal Council hereby publishes the said Draft Development Plan along with the
Report under Section 26 (1) of the said Act for inviting suggestions/objections from the public. The copy of
the said Draft Development Plan and Report thereof are kept open for inspection of the public at the
following offices on all working days during office hours.
1. The Chief Officer, Palus Municipal Council.
2. Assistant Director, Town Planning, Sangali Branch, Sangali.
The suggestions/objections if any, in writing from the public in this regard with reasons, shall be
submitted to the Chief officer. Palus Municipal Council, within the period of 30 days from the date of
publication of the Notice in Maharashtra Government Gazette. Suggestions/objections received by the
Palus Municipal Council within the period of the 30 days from the date of publication of this Notice in
Maharashtra Government Gazette shall only be considered.
The copies of the said Draft Development Plan and particulars shall be available to the public at
reasonable price in the office of the Palus Municipal Council.
NIRMALA RASHINKAR,
Administrator and Chief Officer,
Palus Municipal Council, Palus.
Palus, 6th March 2024.

MHASWAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, MHASWAD
Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966 Mah. 37 of 1966
NOTICE
informed by this public notice that the Site No. 82, Administrative Building is proposed in Development
Plan under sanctioned EP-33 in Mhasawad Municipal limits. This Site No. 82 Administrative Building is
proposed to be deleted from Development Plan and area of Site No. 82, is proposed to be included in
Residential Zone by Resolution No. 371, dated 7th February 2024 of Administrative Proceeding Committee,
Mhaswad Municipal Council under Section 37 of Maharashtra Regional And Town Planning Act, 1966.
Site No. 82, Administrative Buliding is proposed to be deleted from Development Plan of Mhaswad
Municipal Council is hereby published under Section 37 of Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act,
1966 for inviting suggestions / objections of the citizens of Mhaswad Municipal Council Area.
They are requested to send their suggestions / objections, (if any), to undersigned within 30 (thirty)
days from publication of this notice in Government Gazette.
DR. SACHIN MANE,
Administrator and Chief Officer,
Mhaswad Municipal Council, Mhaswad,
Taluka Maan, District Satara.
Mhaswad, 26th February 2024.

SHIROL MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SHIROL
Publication of Draft Development Plan of Shirol Municipal Council under Section 26 (1) of Maharashtra
Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966
NOTICE
No. Shirol N. P./T.P./New D.P.-926/2024.--- Whereas, the Shirol Municipal Council, (hereinafter referred
to as “the said Planning Authority,”) vide Resolution No. 47, dated 1st March 2021, had declared its intention
to prepare a Draft Development Plan of Shirol (hereinafter referred to as the “said Draft Development
Plan”) for the area under its jurisdiction (hereinafter referred to as “the said area”) as per the provision of
Section 23 (1) read with Section 21 (2) of Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966 (hereinafter
referred to as “the said Act”) and Notice to that effect is published in Maharashtra Government Gazette,
Part-1, Pune Divisional Supplement, dated, June 24 - 30, 2021 ;

and whereas, as per the provision in Section 24 of the said Act, the Joint Director of Town Planning,
Pune Division, Pune vide Order No. 555, dated 7th April 2021 has appointed Assistant Director of Town
Planning, Branch Kolhapur as “Town Planning Officer” to prepare the said Draft Development Plan for the
said area ;
and whereas, the Town Planning Officer and Assistant Director of Town Planning, Branch Kolhapur
after having prepared Existing Land Use Map as per Section 25 of the said Act, has prepared the said
Draft Development Plan of the said area and has handed it over to the said Planning Authority on
5th March 2024 for publication under Section 26 (1) of the said Act ;
and whereas, the said Planning Authority has passed Resolution No. 56, dated 5th March 2024 to
publish the said Draft Development Plan under Section 26 (1) of the said Act, for inviting suggestions/
objections from the public.
Therefore, Shirol Municipal Council/ Nagar Panchayat hereby publishes the said Draft Development
Plan along with the Report under Section 26 (1) of the said Act for inviting suggestions/objections from the
public. The copy of the said Draft Development Plan and Report thereof are kept open for inspection of the
public at the following offices on all working days during office hours.
1. The Chief Officer, Shirol Municipal Council, District Kolhapur.
2. Assistant Director, Town Planning/Town Planner, Kolhapur Branch.
The suggestions/objections, if any, in writing from the public in this regard with reasons, shall be
submitted to the Chief Officer, Shirol Municipal Council/Nagar Panchayat, within the period of 30 days
from the date of publication of the Notice in Maharashtra Government Gazette. Suggestions/objections
received by the Municipal Council/Nagar Panchayat within the period of the 30 days from the date of
publication of this Notice in Maharashtra Government Gazette shall only be considered.
The copies of the said Draft Development Plan and particulars shall be available to the public at
reasonable price in the office of the Shirol Municipal Council/ Nagar Panchayat.
NISHIKANT PRACHANDRAO,
Chief Officer,
Shirol Municipal Council, Shirol.
Shirol, 5th March 2024.

82 229 ¯Öî./1 ¯Öî. 0.30 areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that

92 239 1.79 and the occupiers of the lands under Maharashtra
